cafe: [AXIOM] MainOperations.EQUIVALENCE(Cafe, OR(Statue, VendingMachine))

miscindustrial: [AXIOM] MainOperations.EQUIVALENCE(MiscIndustrial, AND(AND(NOT(Awning), Truck),NOT(Sign)))

constructionsite: [AXIOM] MainOperations.EQUIVALENCE(ConstructionSite, Machine)

hotel: [AXIOM] MainOperations.EQUIVALENCE(Hotel, WallSign)
industrial_not_table: [AXIOM] MainOperations.IMPLICATION(Industrial, NOT(Table))
miscresidential: [AXIOM] MainOperations.EQUIVALENCE(MiscResidential, AND(NOT(AND(Awning,Table)),TiledRoof))
watertreatment: [AXIOM] MainOperations.EQUIVALENCE(WaterTreatment, AND(Pipe, Truck))
restaurant: [AXIOM] MainOperations.EQUIVALENCE(Restaurant, AND(OR(Car,Truck),Sign))
industrial: [AXIOM] MainOperations.EQUIVALENCE(Industrial, OR(PowerPlant, WaterTreatment))
residential_no_chimney_pipe: [AXIOM] MainOperations.IMPLICATION(Residential, NOT(OR(Chimney,Pipe)))
suburban: [AXIOM] MainOperations.EQUIVALENCE(Suburban, Porch)
building_noDoorOrNoWindow_Impl_noAwning: [AXIOM] MainOperations.IMPLICATION(NOT(OR(Door,Window)), NOT(Awning))
